# Final world evaluation (159 countries; final freeze D8, 2026-11-20T14:23:39Z)

Generated by `tests/tools/final_world.py` from `LEDGER.jsonl`, `tests/frozen/D8/`, world/STATUS.md and the registry. Every registered world software grid and dataset with cases was scored once on the final freeze, holdouts included. Cells: exact / within 1 % / covered, statute mode, M1, unless stated. Order of testing (TESTING.md 6): software first (W1-W4), datasets after (D1-D8).

## Headline

- **D8:** all 159 world models are built (A 76, B 78, C 5) and frozen; all run through the harness. LC is exact (400/400) in convention mode with the IRD's cited allowance code. IR's open-source 1404 calculator (single author) agrees within 1 % on every case (rounding to tomans). AL's example is relabelled 2024 (a test fix: Ligj 9/2025 from 13 March 2025). Datasets: holdout 23,143 / 27,227 / 40,375 covered.

- **D7:** the rescued models are frozen (TG AO KP VA at grade B, PG at A). New official-calculator grids, run in a Docker sandbox: PH 400/400 exact, XK 400/400; LC 90 of 400 exact, because the IRD's standard code allows 25,400 against the model's 25,000. PW is 15/15 after a test fix and SO 3/3 after a model fix. KH's leaflet: 2/2. CM's `--dgi-bareme` convention halves the IRPP gap but is not exact (14 of 4,092). Datasets: holdout 23,075 / 27,107 / 40,256 covered.

- **D6 (the D6 hunts):** 40 proposals registered before reading. 22 datasets for 12 new countries are scored: BW 1,174/1,174, CM TDL 3,135/3,135, BB 359/359, DO 182/182 (dev, not independent), DZ 763/776, PH 10/10 (dev) and RW, UG, ZM all exact. CM IRPP is 239 exact of 4,092: the model is about 14 % above the DGI barème. PW, SO and MA are small sets that differ. Datasets: holdout 22,755 / 26,720 / 39,854 covered. The remaining D6 material (EC BR AL FJ BO TT, OCR for KH and MM, the six client-side calculators) is registered and open for D7.

- **D5:** the four rescued grade-A models (KH, LA, MD, PS) are built and frozen. No referee exists for any of them: no software; KH's GDT pages are script-rendered, LA's tax site does not resolve, MD's State Tax Service returns 403, PS's finance ministry fails TLS verification (none bypassed). Every score is as in D4.

- **D4:** BT now implements income year 2026. Its DRC withholding tables score 17 exact / 48 within 1 % of 299. Every case up to Nu 1,000,000 of gross is within 1 %. Above that the gap is Rule 153's uncapped 15 % monthly withholding against the annual s. 44 cap: a withholding convention, not a statute error. 152 models are built (KH, LA and MD rescued, grade C, no referee).

- **D3 (dataset hunt):** 122 countries searched. Converted: SB (IRD monthly PAYE tables, holdout: 1,032 of 1,032 exact), ZW (ZIMRA 2025 table, holdout: 19 of 19 exact), BT (2026 tables, not implemented). Unreachable 35; blocked (403 or script challenge, not bypassed) 16; reachable but no dated referee material 64; rates only or wrong year 4.

- **Models (D8, from tests/frozen/D8 and world/STATUS.md):** all 159 world countries have a built model: grade A 76, grade B 78, grade C 5 (ER GM KI ST VC). All 159 ran through the world harness without a failure.
- **Order of testing:** software first (rounds W1-W4), then datasets (D1-D2), as Alan ordered.
- **Software phase:** 40 grids for 22 countries.
  - Final (D2) dev: 26,365 exact / 35,328 within 1 % of 53,644 (49 % exact, 66 % within 1 %).
  - Holdout: 11,098 / 14,924 of 22,856.
  - The first scores of these grids, before any fix (W3, the 40 grids), were 25,373 / 36,291 dev and 10,698 / 15,322 holdout.
- **Dataset phase:** 6 countries with covered cases (CL CO CR IL TR DM). JM's 3 cases are registered but not implemented after the D2 test fix.
  - Dev: 423 exact / 530 within 1 % of 777 covered (5,370 cases).
  - Holdout: 16,245 / 19,386 of 29,181 covered (46,412 cases).
  - In convention mode (CL and CO `--oecd`, citations audited) the OECD holdouts reach 7,177 / 8,154 of 11,296.
- **No referee at all:** after D8, 113 of the 159 countries have neither a software grid nor a covered dataset case (132 after D2). 46 have a referee: 22 by the 40 software grids, 25 by datasets, official calculators or the IR open-source calculator (CL CO CR IL TR DM SB ZW BT; BB BW CM DO DZ MA PH PW RW SO UG ZM; KH LC XK IR), IL by both. AL's only case is a 2024 example since D8. Per-country reasons: world/HUNT_D3.md. Their models are built from the statute and checked only by their own `#guard`s and theorems.

## What improved and what did not

- **Improved, from the statute** (each fix cites its dated text; ROUNDS.md, World):
  - SG CPF above age 55 (W2): dev exact 339 -> 429.
  - BD tax-free threshold under the Finance Act 2026 (W4): both BD tools 118 -> 2,109 of 2,110 exact.
  - IL National Insurance ceiling and child allowance (D2): Taxing Wages 2025 62 -> 69 of 80; decomposition holdout 2,793 -> 3,243 exact.
- **Improved by test fixes, not model gains:**
  - PK, SN, TN and SG grids rebuilt (W2): the grid compared the wrong line or left the package's family inputs unset.
  - PK and PK TaxBacha tax-year labels.
  - The JM dataset's month label (D2).
- **Fell, deliberately: ID (W4).** The compulsory employee JHT and JP contributions are now deducted (PP 45-46/2015). The model moved away from three single-author tools:
  - id-utils ignores the contributions: exact 1,038 -> 40.
  - Peco and gajian add the employer half, which the model does not yet: within 1 % 958 -> 43 and 348 -> 95.
  - The fourth tool, indo-salary, gained (within 1 % 188 -> 504).
  - ID's software total fell from 1,178 to 179 exact of 7,384. Reported as a regression; the employer half is the open item.
- **Did not move:**
  - NG (0 exact on three tools: their relief base is wrong) and PY (0: the package's 9.5 % IPS rate).
  - KE (15 and 12 exact: the NSSF upper earnings limit has no official 2025 figure).
  - VN (327 of 2,072: decrees not reached).
  - CN contributions (no citable city default).
  - DM's IRD table (2 of 941; holdout, now released). Whether the 2018 table still applies in 2025 was the test agent's judgement.
- **Held-out lines:**
  - Software grids: holdout exact rose 10,698 -> 11,098, almost all from BD.
  - OECD decomposition: holdout exact rose only through IL (statute) and through CL and CO in convention mode. Their statute lines did not move.

## What cannot be tested

- **Grade C:** 5 countries, all built as stubs or partial models (world/RESCUE.md, Pass 2-3). ST: the 2025 budget bill was reached but not the enacted law, so the 2025 IRS table is not certain. VC: Finance Acts 2010-2022 and 2024 are missing, so the 2025 bands and top rate cannot be fixed. GM: the IVAT Act 2012 and the Finance Acts are missing (the GRA 2025 withholding table is kept raw until a graded model exists). KI: the 2022-2025 acts sit in a broken download list, so completeness for 2025 cannot be shown. ER: no tax text online.
- **No referee:**
  - 132 countries have neither a software grid nor a covered dataset case, including the 118 with no open software.
  - The official tables of SR, DO and SV refused plain requests (403); nothing was bypassed.
  - PW returned an empty page; GD failed certificate verification.
  - VC and CU could not be reached; CM, BZ and HT need a browser.
  - TZ's latest table is 2023/24 against a 2025 model. KE, GH and GY publish rates pages without worked examples.
- **Years:** every world model implements one year (2025, or its 2025-26 equivalent). The OECD's 2022-24 Taxing Wages tables (3,648 cases, 1,051 of them holdout) and TaxBEN 2024 are therefore not implemented.
- **Contract:** employer contributions, labour costs and the OECD's intermediate lines are outside the household contract, as for the 38.

## Alan's hypothesis: test on software first, then datasets

- **The evidence is thin and does not support a conclusion either way.**
- Of the 22 software-tested countries, only IL also has a dataset score. IL's software round (one single-author grid) found two errors in the software and none in the model. IL therefore entered the dataset phase unchanged, and its dataset gains came from the dataset phase itself.
- Each phase found errors the other could not have found here:
  - The software phase found three statute errors in 22 countries (SG, BD, ID) and the software errors in the table above. Its grids had households the official tables lack: ages over 55, couples, children, pension contributions.
  - The dataset phase found two (IL, JM) in 7 countries, plus the CL and CO OECD conventions.
- **What orders the dataset results is the model's grade, not its testing history.**
  - The grade-A models (TR, CR) are near-exact on Taxing Wages 2025: 71 of 72 and 62 of 72.
  - The grade-B models are not: IL 69 of 80, CO 28, CL 16. CO and CL reach 59 and 36 only under the OECD's conventions.
- A fair test would need countries with a dataset baseline before and after a software phase that changed their models. No world country has one.

## Holdout release

The final world round is closed. The per-case details of every world holdout scored on D2 are released into dev under TESTING.md rule 7: `tests/released/world_D2/`. The holdout parts and `tests/quarantine/holdout_cases/` are marked RELEASED.
